# Pulse Chat — Worklog (shared handover doc)

Project: **Pulse** — a mobile-only real-time chat system (Next.js 16 App Router + shadcn/ui + Prisma SQLite + socket.io mini service).
Golden rule: **NO mock/hardcoded data anywhere.** All data flows through Prisma DB via REST APIs; realtime via socket.io.

## Architecture & Contracts (established by Task 1 — read carefully)

### Stack
- Next.js 16 dev server on port 3000 (`bun run dev`, logs at `/home/z/my-project/dev.log`). NEVER run `bun run build`. NEVER restart it.
- socket.io mini service in `mini-services/pulse-socket/` on port **3003** (`bun run dev` inside that folder, entry `index.ts`).
- Frontend connects with `io('/?XTransformPort=3003', { path: '/' })` ONLY. Never absolute URLs.
- Next API routes relay broadcasts to the socket service via internal HTTP POST `http://localhost:3003/notify`.
- Colors: emerald primary, zinc neutrals, dark-mode ready. NO indigo/blue chrome.
- Mobile-only UI: full-screen under `sm`; on desktop render a centered phone frame (max-w ~[420px], h-dvh or fixed 850px, rounded frame, subtle shadow).

### Prisma models (already pushed to db/custom.db)
User(id, name, about, color, createdAt, lastSeenAt) · Conversation(id, isGroup, name, createdAt, updatedAt) · ConversationParticipant(userId, conversationId, lastReadAt, @@unique([userId,conversationId])) · Message(id, conversationId, senderId, content, deletedAt, createdAt). See `prisma/schema.prisma`.

### Shared types — single source of truth: `src/lib/types.ts`
AppUser, MessageAuthor, ChatMessage, ConversationSummary, ConversationDetail, PresenceSnapshot, TypingEvent, ReadEvent, SocketMessageEvent. Backend MUST serialize dates as ISO strings matching these interfaces exactly.

### REST API contract (Task 2-a builds under `src/app/api/`)
Next.js 16 quirk: route handler 2nd arg is `{ params: Promise<{id:string}> }` → must `await params`. Always `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` and return `NextResponse.json(...)`. Validate inputs; trim strings; name ≤ 32 chars; message content ≤ 2000 non-empty. Errors: `{ error: string }` with proper status.

1. `POST /api/users` body `{ name, color? }` → creates user → `201 { user: AppUser }`
2. `GET /api/users` → `{ users: AppUser[] }` sorted by name
3. `GET /api/users/[id]` → `{ user }` or 404 (used for session validation)
4. `PATCH /api/users/[id]` body `{ name?, about?, color? }` → `{ user }`
5. `GET /api/conversations?userId=X` → `{ conversations: ConversationSummary[] }` sorted by updatedAt desc. Include all members (with their User rows), lastMessage (incl deleted tombstones, with sender {id,name,color}), unreadCount = count(senderId != X, deletedAt null, createdAt > my lastReadAt). Every member's participant row is needed by UI for read ticks — include each member's `lastReadAt` by merging into members array as `(AppUser & { lastReadAt })[]` in BOTH summary & detail (UI reads it optionally).
6. `POST /api/conversations` body `{ creatorId, memberIds: string[], name?, isGroup? }` → dedupe DM: if !isGroup resolve to existing conversation whose participants set == {creatorId+memberIds} exactly → returns existing; else create (+participants). Group requires ≥3 distinct valid userIds incl creator, defaults name `"Group of N"`. Response `201 { conversation: ConversationSummary }` (same shape as list item).
7. `GET /api/conversations/[id]?userId=X` → `{ conversation: ConversationDetail }` incl messages? NO — messages come from separate endpoint. Detail = meta + members w/ lastReadAt. 404 if missing. userId required to know viewer but detail shape same for all.
8. `GET /api/conversations/[id]/messages?limit=200&after=<ISO>` → `{ messages: ChatMessage[] }` ascending by createdAt, includes soft-deleted (UI renders tombstone), sender relation embedded. `after` optional (fetch-only-newer for polling fallback).
9. `POST /api/conversations/[id]/messages` body `{ senderId, content }` → validate sender is participant (403 else) → create message → touch conversation.updatedAt → bump sender's lastReadAt=now (sender obviously read it) → relay socket (see below) → `201 { message: ChatMessage }`
10. `DELETE /api/messages/[id]` body `{ requesterId }` → only sender may delete (403) → soft delete (set deletedAt) → relay socket message:deleted → `{ message: ChatMessage }`
11. `POST /api/conversations/[id]/read` body `{ userId }` → upsert participant.lastReadAt=now → relay socket read event → `{ ok: true }`

### Socket mini service contract (Task 2-b builds `mini-services/pulse-socket/`)
Bun project, own package.json (`bun add socket.io` already done at root, but the mini service needs its OWN install), deps: socket.io ^4.8. Script: `"dev": "bun --hot index.ts"`, port hardcoded 3003, httpServer pattern per `/home/z/my-project/examples/websocket/server.ts` (path '/', cors '*', ping settings).

Client→Server events:
- `join { userId }` → tracks presence Map<userId, Set<socketId>>, joins room `user:{userId}`, updates User.lastSeenAt via... NO DB ACCESS from this service (avoid dual prisma across processes). Instead respond + broadcast:
  - to everyone: `presence:snapshot { onlineUserIds }` when anyone joins/leaves
  - ack to joining socket: `joined { onlineUserIds }`
Server→Client (relayed from Next API): `message:new`, `message:deleted`, `message:read` (payload ReadEvent), plus direct client→client relays:
- `typing { conversationId, userId, userName, isTyping }` client emits with recipientIds → server forwards payload to those rooms except sender's own sockets.

Internal HTTP relay endpoint (called by Next API routes):
- `POST /notify` JSON body `{ event: 'message:new'|'message:deleted'|'message:read', recipients: string[], payload: unknown }` → io.to(`user:{r}`).emit(event, payload) for each recipient id. Response `{ ok, delivered: n }`.
- `POST /typing` JSON body `{ recipients, ...TypingEvent }` → emit `typing` payload to recipients.

Presence semantics: a user is ONLINE while ≥1 of their sockets is connected. On disconnect of last socket → offline + re-broadcast snapshot. Maintain memory map userId→Set(socketId).

### Frontend contract (Task 3 builds UI)
- Session: zustand store persisted to **sessionStorage** key `pulse.session.v1` `{ user: AppUser | null }` (per-tab identity, enables multi-account testing). Store actions setUser/clearUser. On boot: if stored, GET /api/users/[id]; on 404 clear.
- TanStack Query v5, QueryClientProvider in client providers tree. Keys: `['users']`, `['me', id]`, `['conversations', myId]`, `['conversation', convId]`, `['messages', convId]`.
- Socket lifecycle in a React context provider; auto-join with my userId on connect; expose helpers sendTyping(convId, isTyping); maintain state: onlineIds:Set<string>, typingByConv: Record<convId, Record<userId,{name,isTyping}>> (auto-expire after 4s w/o refresh), plus push incoming `message:new|deleted` into query cache via queryClient.setQueryData on ['messages'] and invalidate ['conversations'], handle `message:read` by updating member.lastReadAt caches; play haptic `navigator.vibrate?.(15)` on receiving when tab hidden or not viewing that convo.
- Screens (single page app state machine, NOT routes): Onboarding → Tabs(Chats | Contacts | Profile) → ChatRoom overlay (full-screen slide-in via framer-motion AnimatePresence) → NewChat sheet/dialog over Contacts tab with group-builder toggle.
- Send flow: optimistic — append temp message immediately, replace with server response; on error toast + keep red state mark.
- Read flow: entering/opening ChatRoom & new messages arrive while open & visible → POST /read (throttle 800ms).
- All copy/en-US. Empty states with generated illustration `/public/onboarding-hero.png` and `/public/empty-chats.png`.

### Files owned per task (do not touch others')
- 2-a: `src/app/api/**`, nothing else (may READ src/lib/db.ts, src/lib/types.ts)
- 2-b: `mini-services/pulse-socket/**`, nothing else
- 3: `src/app/page.tsx`, `src/components/chat/**`, `src/hooks/**` additions OK (`use-pulse-socket.ts`), may edit `src/app/layout.tsx` metadata title/description + viewport, `src/lib/pulse-store.ts`

Agent: full-stack-developer
Task: REST API routes

Work Log:
- Created src/lib/serializers.ts: mapUser/mapAuthor/mapMessage/mapMember DTO mappers (all dates .toISOString()), buildConversationSummary(viewer) w/ per-viewer unread count + members incl lastReadAt watermark sorted by name, buildConversationDetail, CONVERSATION_FULL_INCLUDE reusable Prisma include (participants+users+newest message), memberIdsOf(), safeJson() body parser, strField(), normalizeColor() w/ emerald fallback, parseIsoDate(), validation constants (name≤32, about≤140, msg≤2000, group name≤48, messages limit 200/default 500/max), notifySocket() relay helper → POST http://localhost:3003/notify with 2.5s timeout, silent catch.
- Implemented all 7 route files under src/app/api/** with Next 16 `{ params: Promise<{id}> }` await signature + `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` everywhere.
- POST /api/users: trim + 1..32 name validation → 400; invalid color → emerald; 201 {user}. GET /api/users: name asc.
- GET/PATCH /api/users/[id]: 404 unknown id; PATCH validates provided fields only (name 1..32, about 1..140 trimmed, color normalized), always bumps lastSeenAt=now, validation(400) checked before existence(404).
- GET /api/conversations?userId=X (required): participations → one batched fetch of convs incl members(+users)+latest message each → summaries built concurrently; unreadCount = count(senderId≠me, deletedAt null, createdAt > my lastReadAt); sort updatedAt desc.
- POST /api/conversations: distinct set creator+memberIds, all ids must exist (400); DM needs exactly 2 distinct → dedupes via candidates scan (isGroup=false having exactly both participants, works reverse-direction too) → 200 {conversation} if found else create → 201. Group ≥3 distinct + optional trimmed name ≤48 (400 over), default "Group of N"; always creates new.
- GET /api/conversations/[id]?userId=X (userId required): detail meta + members w/ lastReadAt; 404 unknown.
- GET /api/conversations/[id]/messages?limit=&after=: limit int clamp 1..500 default 200 (non-int→400), after strict ISO (invalid→400), asc order, soft-deleted included, sender {id,name,color}.
- POST /api/conversations/[id]/messages: senderId+content validation (400 empty/>2000), convo missing→404, non-participant→403; $transaction{ create message + touch conversation.updatedAt=now (explicit @updatedAt write allowed by Prisma) + bump sender participant lastReadAt=now }; relay message:new envelope {type,message,recipientIds(excl sender),conversationId} to others; 201 {message}.
- DELETE /api/messages/[id] {requesterId}: missing requesterId→400, unknown→404, already-deleted→200 no-op returns tombstone, non-sender→403; soft delete deletedAt=now; relay message:deleted to other members; {message}.
- POST /api/conversations/[id]/read {userId}: missing→400, convo→404, non-participant→403; upsert-safe update lastReadAt=now; relay message:read ReadEvent to everyone except reader; {ok:true}.

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints live & curl-verified end-to-end against running dev server: user create (trim/color fallback/name bounds), list asc, get/404, patch partial+lastSeenAt bump+empty-about 400; DM create + reverse-direction dedupe (same id, 200 vs 201), DM bad-count 400, ghost-user 400, self-DM 400; group named/default/too-small(<3)/>48-name 400, always-new (no group dedupe) 201; message send 201 (+updatedAt bump confirmed via list ordering, +sender self-read bump confirmed: B's own reply cleared A's pending unread as expected), content/404/non-participant-403 guards; history asc + after ISO filter excludes older rows + invalid limit/after 400 + tombstones included; soft delete sets deletedAt, double-delete 200 no-op, non-sender 403; read endpoint bumps member.lastReadAt and clears unread 1→0; guard codes 400/403/404; malformed JSON body → 400 via safeJson.
- Unread formula verified precisely: viewer counts others' non-deleted msgs newer than own lastReadAt; member watermarks surface in summary.members[].lastReadAt and detail.members[].
- Socket relay calls return cleanly while port 3003 is down (fetch fails → swallowed) — zero impact on API responses; ready for Task 2-b's /notify.
- Query pattern for lists avoids N+1 on conversations/participants/users/lastMessage (single batched findMany per table, logged in dev.log); only unreadCount runs per-conversation inside Promise.all (accepted scale).
- Contract deviations: none functional. Judgment calls documented: about validated 1..140 when provided; provided-but-invalid color PATCH falls back to emerald (mirrors POST rule); provided group name >48 chars rejected 400 rather than truncated; read/messages routes check conversation existence before membership so wrong-id yields 404 not 403.
- Test data (TestA/B/C/D, DM, 2 groups, 4 messages incl 1 tombstone) fully deleted post-verification; DB now empty (GET /api/users → []). Schema/prisma folder untouched; left placeholder src/app/api/route.ts untouched as instructed.

Task ID: 2-b
Agent: full-stack-developer
Task: socket.io mini service

Work Log:
- Created mini-services/pulse-socket/ with own package.json ("dev": "bun --hot index.ts", dep socket.io ^4.8.3 → installed 4.8.3, own lockfile)
- IMPORTANT source-audit finding (engine.io@6.6.9): because path is '/', attach()'s interceptor check `req.url.startsWith('/')` ALWAYS succeeds → engine swallows EVERY http request and only falls back to pre-existing createServer handlers on path mismatch. The naive dual-listener pattern (handler + early-return '/socket.io') therefore NEVER fires our /notify and/or double-writes responses (ERR_HTTP_HEADERS_SENT crash risk). 
- Solution implemented in index.ts: attach io normally to bare createServer(), capture the engine-registered 'request'/'upgrade' handlers, removeAllListeners, then register ONE unified dispatcher that (a) delegates any request whose query has EIO+transport back to the captured engine handlers untouched (handshake/polling/websocket-upgrade all verified working through this delegation), (b) serves OPTIONS 204+CORS, POST /notify, POST /typing, health JSON for everything else (incl GET /)
- io() options copied exactly from examples/websocket/server.ts: path '/', cors origin '*' methods GET+POST, pingTimeout 60000, pingInterval 25000 (+ serveClient:false)
- Presence: onlineUsers Map<userId,Set<socketId>> + reverse socketUser map; join {userId} validates non-empty trimmed string (≤64), enters room user:{id}, acks 'joined {onlineUserIds}', always io.emit('presence:snapshot') afterwards; disconnect drops mappings and broadcasts snapshot ONLY when last socket of that user closes; re-join under new id cleans old mapping defensively
- Relays: client 'typing' {recipients,conversationId,userId,userName,isTyping} sanitized (unique trimmed strings, sender excluded); POST /notify whitelist message:new|message:deleted|message:read, recipients array required, payload passthrough; both respond {ok:true,delivered:n}, 400 on bad body/event; body cap 64KB
- Health probe GET anything-unknown → {ok:true,service:'pulse-socket',port,online,uptimeSec}; uncaughtException/unhandledRejection logged-but-survivable (resilient long-running relay); SIGTERM/SIGINT graceful close like example
- Started via nohup bun run dev → service.log; verified port 3003 LISTEN stable single PID

Stage Summary:
- Events supported: join→{joined,presence:snapshot}; typing relay; disconnect presence transitions; server→client message:new|message:deleted|message:read via /notify; multi-tab same-user collapses to one presence entry until LAST tab closes
- Endpoints: WS/SIO on '/' ; POST /notify, POST /typing (JSON, CORS *, OPTIONS 204), GET * health JSON
- Verification: curl health ok; /notify & /typing empty-recipients → {"ok":true,"delivered":0}; bad event/bad JSON → 400; bun socket.io-client smoke test (polling+ws): joined acks contain correct ids, B received targeted message:new while A didn't, typing relayed with exact TypingEvent shape, offline transition broadcast 2→1 observed by peer, graceful SIGTERM clean exit; eslint on index.ts clean; no crashes in service.log (Task 2-a agent already successfully delivered a message:deleted through /notify during integration — cross-task contract confirmed)

Agent: Z.ai Code orchestrator

Work Log:
- STATUS ASSESS: all services green at round start (keeper respwaned dev once earlier, socket 7121s uptime). agent-browser regression PASSED: onboarding → register (Milo/teal) → contacts (9 users incl. real signups JOhn/bulu) → DM open → send → "sent" tick (correct: peer offline) → persistence via API. Baseline screenshot saved.
- CRITICAL TOOLING DISCOVERY (must-read for future rounds): the sandbox command/display pipeline EATS literal `[m` byte sequences (ANSI-escape filter artifact) — BOTH on input (my command text) and output (tool results). This masqueraded as "file corruption" in schema.prisma (Reaction model looked mangled) — python boolean checks proved files on disk were FINE; earlier Edit-tool failures were input-side mangling of old_str. RULES: (1) never trust visual output containing `[m`-adjacent text; verify via python `'pattern' in content` checks; (2) construct bracket-heavy strings at runtime (chr(91)); (3) trust `bunx tsc --noEmit` + `prisma validate` as ground truth. (Also explains round-3's phantom `const enuOpen` incident.)
- Schema: Message.audioPath String? + durationMs Int? → db push + client regen → killed dev child, keeper auto-respawned fresh client (runbook rule followed).
- PAGINATION (fixes real scalability bug): GET messages previously returned OLDEST 200 asc; now returns NEWEST-200 window asc + hasMore + total (count). Added `before=<ISO>` cursor branch (desc take N → reversed asc) for older pages. Client: queryFn MERGES fetched window with cached pages (dedupe by id, sort asc) so background refetches (3.5s) never amputate loaded history; "Load older messages" pill (ChevronUp, spinner while pending) at list top; scroll-anchor via useLayoutEffect + scrollRestoreRef armed ONLY at data-arrival (first attempt had a bug where the loading-state render consumed the anchor — caught and fixed). hasMore computed as cachedCount < server total (robust vs refetch clobbering — found via E2E: pill reappeared after exhaustion before this fix).
- GROUP MANAGEMENT: PATCH /api/conversations/[id] {requesterId,name} (group-only 400, participant 403, name 1..48) → relay conversation:updated; POST /api/conversations/[id]/members {requesterId,userIds[]} (group-only, user existence 400, dup-skip, lastReadAt=now no backlog, touch updatedAt) → relay; DELETE same route (leave group, self-removal only) → relay to remaining. Socket svc NOTIFY_EVENTS + 'conversation:updated' (bun --hot auto-reload). Realtime provider: onConversationUpdated → invalidate ['conversation',id] + ['conversations'].
- InfoDialog overhaul: groups get pencil-rename (inline input, Enter/Esc/save/cancel), "Add members" → in-dialog picker (users query key ['users'] shared w/ contacts cache, excludes current members, checkbox rows, "Add N" submit), "Leave group" → AlertDialog confirm ("Stay"/"Leave group"). Renames/members patch the detail cache directly + toast; leave closes room + invalidates lists.
- VOICE NOTES: uploads POST accepts audio data URLs (webm/mpeg/ogg/wav/mp4/aac → file ext mapped, mp4→m4a) returning {filePath, imagePath alias}; serve route whitelist + audio mimes. POST messages accepts audioPath (AUDIO_EXT_REGEX + on-disk check) + durationMs (0..600000, orphan-duration 400). Composer: mic button (empty input only — send button morphs back when typing); tap → getUserMedia → MediaRecorder (opus candidates w/ fallback) → recording bar (pulsing red dot, tabular timer, X cancel / emerald send-stop), <600ms discarded, chunk-safe base64 → upload → send w/ durationMs; unmount safety discards. VoiceBubble: play/pause circle, deterministic 26-bar waveform (hashString-seeded LCG — decorative, not real PCM), white/emerald progress fill via timeupdate, mm:ss duration. Jumbo-emoji logic excludes audio msgs. types.ts ChatMessage +audioPath/durationMs; socket validator carries them.
- Chats list: typing indicator in rows (animated 3-dot emerald italic "typing…" replaces preview, driven by realtime.typersIn — destructured for react-compiler memo lint); voice preview "🎤 Voice message"; unread badge +ring/shadow pop; date chip +shadow/ring depth; UNREAD divider label pill-tinted; empty room state + gradient icon tile.
- INFRA: keeper hardened after a false-positive respawn storm during 12-worker bulk seeding (probe timeouts under load → 16 spawn attempts died on port-in-use; real server never died): now requires 3 consecutive failed probes + 15s cooldown after fast-exit spawn + exposes downStreak/cooldownSec in health. Hot-reloaded clean.

- Browser E2E (real session injection: sessionStorage pulse.session.v1 ← original Alice Chen — NOTE: onboarding "Start chatting" ALWAYS creates a NEW user, names are not unique; ?login= prefill ≠ account selection): voice preview + 🎤 in list ✓; >200-msg conv → Load older pill ✓ → click → 206/206 msgs, scroll anchor EXACT (top=272 = newH-prevH+prevTop) ✓ → pill permanently gone incl. after refetches ✓; group rename live in dialog+list ✓; picker select 2 → "5 members" ✓; leave confirm → toast + room closes + gone from list + server-truth verified ✓; voice bubble light+dark screenshots (play btn, waveform, 0:02/0:03 durations) ✓; mic in headless → NotFoundError → error-toast path ✓ (real recording needs human thumb-test, standard MediaRecorder API).
- CLEANUP: bulk QA conversation (205 msgs) deleted, 2 stray duplicate users deleted, orphan silent wav file removed. Kept: Bob→Alice 2.2s tone voice demo (dd1019cd...wav), "Alpine Club 🏔️" group (Bob/Cara/Dora/Milo), all prior fixtures.

One-shot backfill: each legacy group's earliest-message author promoted ('Alpine Club 🏔️'→Bob, 'QA Read Stack'→Bob). Types: GroupRole export, ConversationDetail.members now carry role. serializers: mapRole() normalizer + mapMember includes role (summaries inherit harmlessly).

DELETE members (leave): if leaver is SOLE admin and members remain → auto-promotes longest-standing survivor (joinedAt asc, id asc tiebreak) in one tx; returns promotedUserId. conversations POST create: creator's participant row gets role='admin' for groups. curl-QA'd EVERY path: non-admin rename/add/manage→403, bad action→400, self-kick→400, sole-admin self-demote→400, promote/demote cycle 200 with role flips, kick member 200, creator-admin on fresh group, leave-succession promotedUserId verified. Throwaway group + temp user cleaned up afterwards.

ADMIN view as live CREATOR ("R9 Scout" created "Scout Squad" w/ Bob+Cara via New-group sheet): own row "(you) Admin" no controls; per-other-row Promote/Remove buttons w/ aria-labels; Promote Bob click → header "2 admins", chip moves, button becomes Demote (qa-r9-admin-promote.png); Demote click → back to "1 admin"; Remove opens confirm AlertDialog (name interpolation correct, Cancel aborts clean, qa-r9-remove-confirm.png); Rename+Add-members buttons present for admin. LEAVE-with-succession in real UI: sole admin left → toast, dialog closes, session lands on empty state, DB shows survivor auto-promoted (Bob admin). All roles restored to fixtures (QA Read Stack: Alice member/Bob admin/Cara member).
- SHIPPED 2 - SWIPE-REPLY AFFORDANCE POLISH (mandatory styling detail): prior shipped swipe had hardConstraints {0,0}+elastic .24 and a Reply indicator hardcoded opacity-0 (gesture existed but was INVISIBLE). Now: useMotionValue bubbleX bound via style, dragConstraints ±56px w/ elastic .16 + dragDirectionLock; indicator spans animate opacity [10..34px]→[0..1] and scale .6→1.05, emerald pill bg-emerald-500/10; single derived signal towardX = mine? -x : x so wrong-direction drags keep hints hidden; threshold trigger (>52) unchanged. Rest-state rendering unchanged (opacity 0 at rest = safe regression).
- CRITICAL OPS GOTCHA (round 8 runbook refinement): kill -9 of the `next/dist/bin/next` WRAPPER pid does NOT restart the server — `start-server.js` child keeps :3000 and keeps serving the OLD prisma client (stale-role API responses + silent empty-body 500s from new code). MUST kill the LISTENER pid (ss -tlnp | grep 3000); keeper respawns a fully fresh process. This wasted a full debug cycle earlier in the round.
- GESTURE AUTOMATION LIMITATION (documented honestly): pixel-drag/long-press E2E via mouse events blocked this sandbox round by React delegation subtleties (delegated handlers need event path INSIDE bubble subtree; wrapper/gutter targets miss them) plus stale leftover dialogs intercepting hit-tests mid-run; previous rounds also needed elementFromPoint tricks. Swipe logic itself is build+tsc verified; its interactive branch (beginReply→onReply machinery) predates this round unchanged. Long-press manual UX unaffected (menu machinery untouched).

Zero schema changes this round; _queued is a client-only optional field.
- KEY NOTES for next rounds: (1) terminal `cat` of files can drop the literal "[m" byte pair — if a file "looks" corrupted via Bash, re-check with Read before editing; (2) ChatMessage._queued may appear on cached temp messages only — any future cache consumer should keep treating temp- ids as non-interactive (MessageRow already does via pending); (3) flushPulseOutbox lives in pulse-outbox.ts and takes the QueryClient as arg — do not import db/server stuff there; (4) offline queue is TEXT-ONLY by design (uploads need network) — composer photo/mic paths still error-toast naturally when offline; (5) agent-browser CLI in this sandbox has NO `offline` command — stub via Object.defineProperty(navigator,'onLine')+dispatchEvent, and remember to restore (defineProperty back to true + 'online' event).
- Risks/notes: queued messages aren't long-press interactive (can't reply/react/delete a not-yet-sent msg — matches pending behavior); leaving a room while messages are queued keeps temp bubbles in the in-memory cache (fine) but a cache-miss reopen offline shows history sans queued bubbles until flush (cosmetic edge, self-heals on reconnect); outbox entries carry sender snapshot — identity spoofing impossible (server validates senderId is a participant, but a local localStorage edit could queue under another participant id — single-profile browser assumption, same trust level as existing session store).
- Next-phase candidates: offline queue for images/voice (persist dataURL, upload on flush) — outbox badge chip on chat rows ("N queued" mini pill) — per-message delivery info for others' messages — archive bulk-select — FTS5 search index — quiet-hours server sync — PWA install prompt E2E on a real device profile.

Work Log:

- KEY API-VERIFICATION FINDING (resolves the brief's ambiguity): collection DELETE /api/conversations/[id]/members is LEAVE-ONLY — it reads only `requesterId` and silently IGNORES any `userId` (sending {requesterId, userId} there would kick the ADMIN THEMSELVES, not the target — destructive misfire, not a 400). Admin-remove is served by the dedicated subroute DELETE /api/conversations/[id]/members/[userId] body { requesterId } (chat-room already uses it): 400 "Use \"Leave group\" to remove yourself from this chat." on self-kick, 403 "Admins cannot be removed from the group." on admin targets. Remove-member UI implemented against the subroute with destructive confirm + graceful toasts; the menu item is hidden for admin targets (server 403s anyway).
- Promote/demote uses PATCH /api/conversations/[id]/members { requesterId, userId, role } exactly as briefed; last-admin guard toasted verbatim ("Cannot demote the last admin — promote someone else first."), 409 "already an/a member" surfaced verbatim too. Optimistic role flip via setQueryData(['conversation', id]) + rollback; settle path INVALIDATES (not caches) because the PATCH detail is built for the TARGET viewer (myMutedUntil correctness).

- KEY PATTERNS for later crews: (1) client-known sets belong in TanStack cache keys (enabled:false observers) — they survive tab-panel unmounts without zustand; (2) fetchQuery(staleTime) gives "hydrate on activation, cached Ns" semantics with zero effects; (3) never materialize false-empty sets (patch returns undefined to bail); (4) invalidate-without-observer marks stale → next mount refetches — safe way to keep optimistic per-app entries from being served fresh; (5) after:-inset-1.5 content-[''] trick extends chip touch targets past 44px without visual bulk.
- KNOWN LIMITS (by design, per brief): tile connect-state is locally-known until first My apps activation or per-app detail fetch (fresh reload shows "Connect" on connected apps — tapping is idempotent upsert, harmless); installers list caps at 6 most recent (server-side).

Task ID: R21-b
Agent: Z.ai Code (pulse "Beyond Chat" wave — live voice rooms)
Task: LIVE walkie-talkie voice rooms (Voxer/Zello style) over pulse-socket :3003 + VoiceRoomSheet + ChatRoom integration

Work Log:
- pulse-socket/index.ts (extended, exclusive): in-memory voice model — `voiceRooms: Map<convId, Map<userId, VoicePeer>>` + `socketVoiceRoom: Map<socketId, convId>` (one voice room per socket), socket.io rooms `voice:{conversationId}`. Client→Server: `voice:join {conversationId, user{id,name,username,color}}` → join room + upsert roster + broadcast `voice:roster {conversationId, peers[]}` (socketIds never leaked); `voice:leave {conversationId?}` → map is authoritative, mismatched claims ignored; `voice:ptt {conversationId, userId, on}` → relayed to WHOLE room incl. sender (self rings glow), gated to the registered peer; `voice:chunk {conversationId, userId, seq, data(base64)}` → relayed to room EXCEPT sender, 96KB cap + identity gate (socket may only stream as its own peer). Disconnect → `leaveVoiceRoom` first: prunes roster, broadcasts fresh roster + force `voice:ptt off` for the departed user (no ghost rings). Health probe now reports `voiceRooms` count. No DB access, existing events (presence/typing/notify/confetti pipeline) untouched.
- NEW src/components/chat/voice-room-sheet.tsx (~990 lines): `useVoiceRoom(conversationId, me)` engine hook + `VoiceRoomSheet` UI. Engine: dedicated socket `io('/?XTransformPort=3003', {path:'/'})` created lazily on join, auto-resync `voice:join` re-emit on reconnect + rate-limited roster-missing-me recovery (relay restart resilience); getUserMedia({echoCancellation, noiseSuppression, autoGainControl}) with honest error mapping (NotAllowed→denied copy, NotFound→no mic, NotReadable→busy); AudioContext created synchronously inside the click gesture (iOS unlock) BEFORE awaits; capture via AudioWorklet from Blob URL (`pulse-capture-processor`) with ScriptProcessor(4096) fallback → 250ms native-rate blocks → linear-interp downsample to 16kHz mono → Int16 → base64 → `voice:chunk` (seq per session, partial window flushed on PTT release); receive side validates payload, drops stale seq, schedules AudioBuffer(16k) playback through a per-peer playhead (max(now+85ms jitter, nextAt)) for gapless-ish output; mute = track.enabled=false + software gate, forcing PTT off; leave/unmount/conv-switch teardown stops tracks, disconnects nodes, closes ctx, emits leave, kills socket. Sheet: framer-motion bottom sheet (spring 380/38, drag="y" velocity>620 or offset>110 dismiss, willChange:transform), zinc-950/95 + white/10 + emerald, roster rows with boxShadow-animated emerald glow rings driven by `voice:ptt` speaking set, big 108px PTT (hold=momentary ≥260ms, tap=latch, pointer-capture + pointerActiveRef so a drifting mouse can't kill a latch, Space/Enter keyboard latch), mute toggle, join/leave buttons, connection line ("Connected via gateway :3003" / "Connecting…" / "Reconnecting…" / "Standby"), inline honest error panel with retry, Esc dismiss, closing sheet force-stops PTT while the engine keeps the room alive.
- chat-room.tsx (surgical, 6 precise edits): import line after slash-palette; `voiceOpen` state + `useVoiceRoom(conversationId, me)` after groupInfoOpen; header mic button (aria-pressed, emerald when live) between PiP and Group-info buttons; floating "Voice · N live" pill (AnimatePresence, spring, emerald ping dot, positioned below header, reopen on tap) rendered only when `voice.inRoom && !voiceOpen`; `<AnimatePresence><VoiceRoomSheet title={headerTitle} myId={me.id} voice={voice} onClose={…}/></AnimatePresence>` mounted in the R19 toolkit overlays block. Composer and all existing behavior untouched.
- VERIFY: pulse-socket restarted (stale :3000-incompatible instance killed; fresh `bun run dev` → uptime resets, `voiceRooms:0` in health). `curl :3003/socket.io/?EIO=4&transport=polling` → handshake sid OK. Node-level socket.io-client roundtrip (temp script, deleted): 7/7 PASS — join→roster self, 2-peer roster broadcast, ptt relay incl. sender, chunk EXCEPT-sender, spoofed userId chunk BLOCKED by identity gate, explicit leave prunes roster, abrupt disconnect → roster cleanup + ptt-off. tsc: 0 errors in voice-room-sheet.tsx / chat-room.tsx / pulse-socket (remaining repo errors are pre-existing in other crews' files: whiteboard route, group-info-sheet WebhooksSection, bot-engine, skills). lint: 0 errors (2 pre-existing unused-directive warnings in stories files). GET / → 200 after edits; dev.log clean (old EADDRINUSE + stories-route 500 entries are historical, not from this task).

Stage Summary:
- SHIPPED: Pulse can now talk. Every conversation header has a mic button opening a dark zinc-950/emerald Live Voice sheet — join with real mic capture (worklet→16k PCM→250ms chunks), hold/tap PTT with emerald glow rings for whoever transmits, mute, roster with "you" marker, honest permission-denied state, and a "Voice · N live" pill that keeps the session alive while the sheet is closed. All audio is relayed live through the socket service and never stored.
- EVENT CONTRACTS (stable for later crews): C→S `voice:join|voice:leave|voice:ptt|voice:chunk`; S→C `voice:roster {conversationId, peers:[{id,name,username,color}]}` / `voice:ptt {conversationId, userId, on}` / `voice:chunk {conversationId, userId, seq, data}` — roster only visible to room members; one voice room per socket; server restart self-heals via client resync.
- KEY PATTERNS: (1) put long-lived session engines in a hook at room scope, sheets are pure UI — enables survive-close badges; (2) AudioContext must be constructed before the first await inside a click handler for iOS; (3) identity-gate every relayed socket event against the in-memory roster (socketId match), else clients can spoof peers; (4) Blob-URL AudioWorklet + ScriptProcessor fallback = zero new public files, universal browser support.
- NEXT (backlog): per-peer speaking level meters from chunk amplitude, voice-room count badge on chat list rows (needs a conversation-level voice:count broadcast), ghost-peephole: replay last 30s for late joiners (opt-in buffer).

Task ID: R21-c
Agent: Z.ai Code (pulse "Beyond Chat" wave — collaborative whiteboard)
Task: In-chat collaborative WHITEBOARD (Zoom/Miro style) — real persisted strokes, delta-poll sync, clear/undo, /whiteboard slash entry

Work Log:
- NEW src/app/api/conversations/[id]/whiteboard/route.ts (GET/POST/DELETE, participant-guarded on every handler via ConversationParticipant lookup; 404 unknown conv, 403 non-member, 400 bad input — all values clamped, no mocks, everything lands in WhiteboardStroke rows):
  · GET ?requesterId=&since=<ISO|epoch ms> → { strokes:[{id,userId,color,width,points:number[][],createdAt:ISO}], serverTime: epochMs, resetAt: epochMs|null }. `since` omitted → full snapshot (fresh joiners); present → createdAt > since (delta), orderBy [createdAt asc, id asc], take 4000. resetAt = latest LogEvent(kind 'whiteboard-clear', message=conversationId).createdAt epoch — REAL queryable clear marker, no schema change.
  · POST body {requesterId, strokes:[{color,width,points:number[][]}]} → 201 {ids:[...], created, serverTime}; batch 1..40 strokes/call, each 2..500 points, coords clamped 0..1, width clamped 0.5..40, color trimmed ≤32 chars; per-stroke create() inside $transaction so real ids return in input order.
  · POST body {action:'undo', requesterId} → 200 {success, removedId, serverTime} — deletes ONLY the caller's most recent stroke (findFirst [createdAt desc, id desc] then delete).
  · DELETE ?requesterId= → 200 {success, reset:true, at: epochMs, cleared: n} — deleteMany all strokes for the conversation + appends LogEvent(kind:'whiteboard-clear', message=conversationId, userId=requesterId) in one $transaction; pollers see resetAt move and wipe.
- NEW src/components/chat/whiteboard-sheet.tsx (~640 lines): full-height dark board sheet (vaul Drawer, h-[97dvh] max-w-[420px] rounded-t-3xl zinc-950 + white/10 borders + emerald accent, Drawer `handleOnly` so only the grab-handle drag-dismisses and the canvas owns pointer events, framer-motion spring entrance on the inner column with willChange:transform). Canvas engine: pointer events with pointerId gating (multi-touch ignored, single active stroke, setPointerCapture), normalized 0..1 coords (resize-safe via ResizeObserver + DPR-aware backing store), live incremental rendering per pointermove (midpoint quad segments + round caps, sub-1.2px jitter filtered), final quadraticCurveTo-midpoint smoothed full redraw on pointerup, taps → round dots (nudged 2-point stroke to satisfy the 2-point minimum), >500-point strokes downsampled keeping last point. Sync: TanStack useQuery refetchInterval 900ms (matches codebase pattern), ?since= watermark = serverTime−1500ms overlap + id-dedupe Set (own echoes skipped), bidirectional pending-stroke adoption when a poll echo races the POST response, canvas is imperative (strokes in refs — zero re-render churn), 2000-stroke cap drops oldest finished. Buttons: 6 colors (emerald/rose/amber/violet/cyan/chalk-white) + 3 widths (2/5/10) as radiogroups with aria-checked, Undo (POST undo → removes removedId locally + redraw; guarded while a stroke is still POSTing), Clear board (two-tap confirm → DELETE → local wipe + watermark jump to `at`−overlap), close button. resetAt change → wipe + re-apply post-clear strokes from the same response. Honest failure paths: unsynced stroke removed + toast, offline/reconnecting status chip, syncing spinner + empty-board hint overlays. Accessibility: canvas role="img" aria-label="Shared whiteboard canvas", all buttons aria-labelled, haptics via pulse-settings. Also exports useWhiteboardSheet(conversationId, meId) → {open, setOpen, node}: the lead's 2-line chat-room wiring (listens for pulse:open-whiteboard, renders the sheet).
- src/components/chat/slash-palette.tsx (EXCLUSIVE, surgical): added `Presentation`-icon entry `{ cmd:'/whiteboard', help:'Open the shared whiteboard', tone:'text-emerald-500' }` right after /location, mirroring the existing row pattern; exported WHITEBOARD_OPEN_EVENT='pulse:open-whiteboard' + dispatchOpenWhiteboard(conversationId?) helper; new optional props onOpenWhiteboard?: () => void and conversationId?: string; internal `activate()` intercepts /whiteboard (prop callback when wired, else window CustomEvent pulse:open-whiteboard with detail {conversationId|null}) then onDismiss — onSelect is NOT called for it so chat-room's plain-text parser never stages "/whiteboard" as text. All other commands flow to onSelect unchanged.
- chat-room.tsx NOT touched (owned by another crew) — see CustomEvent contract below for the lead's post-wave wiring.
- VERIFY: `npx tsc --noEmit | grep whiteboard|slash-palette` → 0 errors (clean). `bun run lint` → 0 errors (2 pre-existing unused-directive warnings in other crews' stories files). A parallel crew's transient chats-tab.tsx duplicate-const compile error briefly 500'd the whole dev server mid-wave (not mine; self-resolved as they edited). curl proofs vs http://localhost:3000 (Alice cmtawq3h4001ktcwn674m1frp, conv cmtawqnzg001mtcwn0h4uwzhr): POST 2 strokes → 201 {"ids":["cmtn1cc60002bnhhcancfqvjp","cmtn1cc60002cnhhc2v5ch1kw"],"created":2}; GET → both strokes back with parsed points arrays; POST undo → 200 removedId=cmtn1cc6…kw; GET → 2→1 stroke; DELETE → {"success":true,"reset":true,"at":1788531282612,"cleared":1}; GET after clear → {"strokes":[],"resetAt":1788531282613} (resetAt null→bumped ✓). Negatives: non-participant → 403; 41 strokes → 400 "send 1-40 strokes per call."; out-of-range input clamped (x:5→1, y:-3→0, width:99→40). Board left clean (final DELETE).

Stage Summary:
- SHIPPED: /whiteboard in the slash palette opens a Miro-grade shared board over any chat — draw with mouse/touch in 6 colors × 3 widths, strokes persist as real WhiteboardStroke rows and delta-sync to every member within ~1s of polling, Undo removes only YOUR last stroke, Clear board wipes it for everyone (two-tap confirm) and pollers auto-wipe via the resetAt marker. Survives close/reopen (full snapshot on join, deltas after).
- API CONTRACT (stable): GET ?requesterId&since(epoch ms|ISO) → {strokes:[{id,userId,color,width,points:number[][],createdAt:ISO}],serverTime:number,resetAt:number|null} (no since = full snapshot) · POST {requesterId,strokes:[{color,width,points}]} → 201 {ids,created,serverTime} (1..40 strokes, 2..500 pts, clamped 0..1) · POST {action:'undo',requesterId} → 200 {success,removedId,serverTime} · DELETE ?requesterId → 200 {success,reset:true,at,cleared} + writes LogEvent(kind='whiteboard-clear', message=conversationId) which IS the resetAt watermark. All handlers participant-guarded.
- CustomEvent CONTRACT for the lead (chat-room.tsx, post-wave): import { useWhiteboardSheet } from '@/components/chat/whiteboard-sheet' and render once per room: `const whiteboard = useWhiteboardSheet(conversationId, me.id)` in ChatRoom body + `{whiteboard.node}` next to the other toolkit overlays. That's the whole listener — the hook subscribes to window event 'pulse:open-whiteboard' (exported const WHITEBOARD_OPEN_EVENT from slash-palette.tsx; dispatched by the palette with detail {conversationId: string|null}, the hook ignores detail and uses its own conversationId). Alternative wiring if preferred: pass onOpenWhiteboard={() => whiteboard.setOpen(true)} + conversationId={conversationId} to <SlashPalette>. Until wired, the palette entry just dismisses (documented, harmless).
- KEY PATTERNS: (1) imperative canvas + refs for strokes, React state only for chrome — no re-render storm while drawing; (2) since-watermark = serverTime−1500ms overlap + id-dedupe absorbs insert/read races without double-drawing; (3) clear = real DB wipe + LogEvent marker row → resetAt is queryable history, no schema change; (4) vaul `handleOnly` is mandatory whenever a full-height drawer contains a drawing/scroll surface, else drag-to-dismiss fights the pointer.
- NEXT (backlog): socket.io push for strokes (drop 900ms latency), per-user live cursors, image export (canvas.toBlob → /api/uploads), pin-board attachment in the room header.

The bot user is REAL and already in the DB: username `pulseai`, name "Pulse AI" (same row the R13 LLM companion uses — one identity, two brains: deterministic engine for commands, LLM for natural chat).
- NEW src/lib/bot-engine.ts — exports `maybeBotReply(conversationId, {id, senderId, content})` (awaited in the send path; whole body try/catch → never crashes a send) + `botWillRespond(content)` (sync predicate). Trigger grammar: leading `/`, `@pulseai` mention (word-boundary regex), or `pulseai,/:!` prefix — after stripping the handle an optional leading `/` is normalized so `@pulseai /roll 20` ≡ `/roll 20`. Unknown bare slash commands stay SILENT (the composer's client-side slash palette owns /effects etc. — no bot noise); handle-mention without command → deterministic "send /help" pointer. Commands, all computed/read server-side: /help (menu), /roll [N] (default 100, cap 10000), /flip 🪙, /8ball <q> (12 classic answers, question quoted), /math (tokenizing recursive-descent parser: + - * / % ** parentheses, unary ±, decimals; NO eval/Function; div-by-zero/non-finite/illegal chars → "Invalid expression"; toPrecision(12) strips float noise), /rps (words + r/p/s aliases), /dice (2d6), /time (Intl en-US UTC), /wallet (REAL caller UserWallet upsert-read → PC/GEM/streak), /poll q | o1 | o2 (|…≤6) — mirrors the poll route's exact creation pattern (message + nested poll.createMany options in one tx, content ''), and respects broadcastMode (bot declines while announcements-only unless admin). Reply insertion `insertBotMessage`: message.create (kind 'text', payload {"bot":true}, TTL expiresAt when conv.ttlSeconds>0) + conversation.updatedAt bump + unarchive-others + notifySocket('message:new', all-members-except-bot) — the exact propagation pattern ai-bot/poll use, so bot replies appear live. Reply-once guard: single-pass engine + bounded (500) repliedFor Set keyed by the user message id.

The gate prevents double replies: a DM with the bot used to answer "/math 2+2" via LLM AND would now double-answer via engine — now exactly one deterministic reply; "@Pulse AI /roll" likewise. All other ai-bot behavior (DM chatter, @Pulse AI mentions, unknown /effects) preserved verbatim.

Task: Pulse premium UI — full-screen particle FX layer + real WebGL liquid-gradient hero + reusable glass primitive + glass polish on spotlight/slash-palette

Work Log:
- NOTE on mount point: the task named `src/app/app-root.tsx`, which does not exist — the real application root is `src/components/chat/app-root.tsx` (mounted by page.tsx, wraps Providers → BootGate). ParticleLayer is mounted EXCLUSIVELY there: ONE import + `<ParticleLayer />` inside <Providers> as a sibling above <BootGate>, nothing else changed in that file. Survives route/tab switches because the root never remounts.
- NEW src/components/fx/particle-layer.tsx — exports `ParticleLayer` (memo, NO props). Single fixed inset-0 canvas, pointer-events-none, z-[95] (above screens z-60/70, below toasts z-100), aria-hidden. Listens on window for PARTICLE_BURST_EVENT ('pulse:particle-burst', detail {x?:0..1,y?:0..1,kind?:'confetti'|'hearts'|'stars'|'burst',count?:number} — contract from src/lib/motion.ts). Canvas2D with globalCompositeOperation='lighter' (additive glow BETWEEN particles — the canvas still composites normally onto the page, so it reads correctly on the light phone frame too). DPR-aware backing store (cap 2×), ResizeObserver-driven resize. rAF loop is fully event-driven: starts on burst, self-stops when the last particle dies (final clearRect) and cancels while document.hidden (visibilitychange resumes). Physics per kind: confetti = upward ±60° cone, gravity 0.16, drag 0.985, rotating rects/ribbons (tumbling height shimmer) in emerald/teal/amber/rose/violet/white; hearts = float-up drift + sine sway, '♥' glyph draw; stars = radial float + 4-point sparkle path with twinkle scale-pulse; burst = radial spark LINES (stroke trailing velocity), fast/short-lived. Lifetime alpha: hold → linear fade. Size 3–7px (hearts 4–8 for glyph legibility). Hard cap 400 simultaneous (splice-from-front drops oldest). prefersReducedMotion() checked per burst → skipped entirely. ZERO React state — canvas + particles + raf id all live in refs/closure; component re-renders never touch the loop.
- NEW src/components/fx/webgl-glow.tsx — exports `WebglGlow` with props { className?: string; intensity?: number = 1 } (intensity feeds a uniform via ref — no pipeline rebuild). Raw WebGL1, no three.js: fullscreen triangle-strip quad + fragment shader with 3 drifting radial blobs (emerald #10b981 0.95×, teal #14b8a6 0.80×, subtle violet 0.38×) over a deep emerald-black base, fbm-ish domain warp (re-samples the blob field at warped UVs), soft filmic curve `1-exp(-col·k)` so overlaps glow without clipping, gentle vignette. Context attrs: {alpha:false, antialias:false, depth:false, stencil:false, powerPreference:'low-power'}. Backing store at 0.5 client resolution, CSS-scaled (gradients survive the upscale). Paused via IntersectionObserver (offscreen) + visibilitychange (hidden); dt clamped 50ms so tab-switches never jump. prefers-reduced-motion → ONE static frame (also redrawn on resize), no loop. Graceful fallback: getContext null / shader or link failure / ANY throw / 'webglcontextlost' (preventDefault) → React-state flip to a CSS radial-gradient div with the identical palette (emerald/teal/violet over dark base). Full cleanup incl. WEBGL_lose_context. Awaiting consumers: onboarding/hero crews render <WebglGlow className="absolute inset-0" /> inside a rounded overflow-hidden wrapper.
- NEW src/components/ui/glass-card.tsx — exports `glassSurface` (raw class string: relative rounded-3xl · border-white/20 dark:border-white/10 hairline · bg-white/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/60 · backdrop-blur-2xl backdrop-saturate-150 · inset top highlight + soft outer shadow, dark variants) and `GlassCard` ({glow?: 'emerald'|'none'} + full HTMLMotionProps<'div'> passthrough so initial/animate/transition ride the shared motion tokens; glow adds a pointer-events-none emerald radial bloom at the top edge). tailwind-merge lets callers override rounded-*/shadow-* per surface.
- src/components/chat/spotlight.tsx (glass polish, behavior untouched): card panel swapped to glassSurface (old flat white/85 + custom shadow removed); entrance now opacity+y:8→0 on shared `spring.soft` (reduced-motion → duration 0); result rows converted to motion.button with 25ms stagger entrance (stagger(i,0.025) + ease.out 0.32s) and whileTap 0.97; prefs.reducedMotion honored for both (initial=false, no whileTap). Message-jump contract untouched: activate() still passes (conversationId, anchorMs, jumpMessageId) to onOpenConversation; keyboard nav/recents/actions identical.
- src/components/chat/slash-palette.tsx (glass polish ONLY): panel swapped to glassSurface (kept rounded-2xl override, willChange); entrance transition now shared `spring.snappy`; rows are motion.button with 25ms stagger + ease.out and whileTap 0.97. R21-c CONTRACT PRESERVED VERBATIM: WHITEBOARD_OPEN_EVENT export, dispatchOpenWhiteboard(), the /whiteboard interception inside activate() (prop callback → else window CustomEvent with detail {conversationId|null} → onDismiss, onSelect NOT called), window-capture keyboard handling, onSelect flow for all other commands.
- Cross-file integration: ParticleLayer consumes the exact event contract in src/lib/motion.ts (PARTICLE_BURST_EVENT / ParticleBurstDetail / ParticleKind / prefersReducedMotion) — any surface can fireParticles({kind, x, y, count}) and it renders; no other wiring needed. No file outside my ownership list was touched.
- VERIFY: `npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | grep "^src/"` → 0 lines. `bun run lint` → clean (0 problems). GET / → 200 (fresh compile, module graph includes the new fx/ui files); dev.log shows no errors from these files (the `db.statusStory.create` 500 at old log position 2744 is a pre-existing R21-d stories-route issue, not mine). No `bun run build`, :3000 never restarted, no agent-browser (lead verifies centrally).

Stage Summary:
- SHIPPED: app-wide particle FX plumbing (canvas layer mounted at the true app root, event-driven, zero idle cost, additive glow, 400-particle cap, reduced-motion aware) · a real raw-WebGL1 liquid-gradient hero component with CSS fallback · a one-import liquid-glass primitive (GlassCard + glassSurface string) · spotlight + slash-palette upgraded to the glass material with shared-token springs/staggers.

(2) WebglGlow — props {className?, intensity?}; renders a filled block (canvas 100% of parent), so size it via the parent/wrapper; safe on light and dark backgrounds (dark liquid field). - RECORDING STATE: status pill now slides in (x−14→0, spring.snappy); the red indicator gained a live pulsing radar ring (motion.span scale 1→2 / opacity 0.85→0 loop, 1.1s, reduced-motion gated) around the dot; timer chip pops in (spring.bouncy); cancel + stop buttons kept (whileTap springs). Existing MediaRecorder flow byte-identical.
- BUBBLES: entrance wrapper motion.div around the drag bubble — ONLY freshly arrived messages animate: justArrived = temp-optimistic OR createdAt > mountMsRef (frozen when historyLoaded commits) AND NOT in landedIdsRef (real ids that just replaced temps — no double-pop on optimistic→real swap); history/older-page loads render static (initial={false}). Own messages land with Telegram squash-stretch (scaleX 1.06/scaleY 0.94, y 6 → 1/1/0, transformOrigin bottom-right); incoming pop scale 0.85/y 8 → 1/0; both spring.bouncy. rowsEqual extended with justArrived + reducedMotion (+ previously-missing onOpenProfile) so the memo never goes stale.
- BUBBLE GESTURES: whileTap 0.97 spring.snappy on every interactive bubble (existing long-press → options dialog untouched); swipe-to-reply retuned per spec — dragConstraints ±64, dragElastic 0.12, dragDirectionLock (vertical scroll untouched), release threshold 52→28px, emerald Reply arrow reveal now fills at 4→28px and scales 0.5→1.1 by 44px, dragMovedRef still suppresses the post-swipe click.
- DATE/UNREAD DIVIDERS: day chips + UNREAD separator now ease.out fade/slide in (opacity/y/scale, reduced-motion gated); day chip glass kept, unread pill gains backdrop-blur.
- SCROLL-TO-BOTTOM: existing "New messages" pill upgraded — spring pop (stiffness 480/damping 20 + whileTap), will-change:transform, and a white count badge showing messages that landed while scrolled away (missed-count effect on messages.data with nearBottomRef guards; ref-mirrored so the scroll handler only setState's on the 1→0 transition, same pattern as the existing setShowJump; resets on jump tap/scroll-to-bottom/room switch; aria-label announces the count).
- REACTION BURSTS: adding a reaction fires fireParticles({kind:'hearts', x/y from the chip's getBoundingClientRect}) — from reaction chips (only when !iReacted, i.e. genuinely adding), from double-tap ❤️ (only when it would ADD, not remove), and from the options-dialog emoji picker (same add-guard); chip count digits pop on change (keyed motion.span, spring.bouncy); hearts burst helper = one synchronous rect read, zero rAF churn.
- TYPING INDICATOR: dots were already framer spring-physics (R19 squash-stretch, stagger 0.14s) — kept; the bubble container upgraded from 0.15s tween to spring.snappy with a slight scale-in.
- EFFECT SENDS: triggerEffectFor now ALSO fires the app-wide particle layer per effect — confetti→confetti, sparkles→stars, lasers/echo→burst — at the composer position (x 0.5, y 0.85, count 90), layered under the existing per-message effect canvas; covers own sends AND incoming effect messages, reduced-motion short-circuits before anything fires.
- PERF CONTRACT: every new animation is transform/opacity only; will-change:transform exclusively on the composer wrapper + jump FAB; no new unguarded setState in scroll handlers; reduced-motion respected on entrance/tap/tray-stagger/radar/pop/particles/dividers.
- REGRESSION SWEEP: voice notes (start/stop/cancel/send), /whiteboard palette entry + sheet wiring, bot pass-through (botCommands set untouched), stickers, location, effects (slash + armed chip + canvas), PiP, threads, polls (builder/vote/close), scheduled sends + chip + drawer, view-once, translations, replies (chip + quote jump), reactions (toggle/info sheet), read receipts, @mentions, slash palette, outbox, drafts — all handlers and flows untouched; only presentation-layer JSX/state added around them. Removed ONLY the old plus-Popover menu (its actions live on as tray tiles).

VERIFY: `npx tsc --noEmit | grep ^src/` → 0 errors repo-wide · `npx eslint src/components/chat/chat-room.tsx` → 0 problems · `bun run lint` → the ONLY error is a concurrent crew's in-flight contacts-tab.tsx set-state-in-effect (not this task's file; chat-room is clean) · GET / → 200, dev.log clean of compile errors · no build, :3000 untouched · zero mocks (every tray tile/button wires an existing real handler).

Agent: full-stack-developer (kanban)
Task: Pulse group kanban board — three-column (todo/doing/done), Prisma-persisted, realtime-synced sheet + REST API (own files only; chat-room/slash-palette/group-info-sheet untouched)

Work Log:
- SCHEMA: consumed the pre-pushed KanbanCard model (conversationId, title, column todo|doing|done, position, assigneeId?, createdById?, @@index([conversationId, column, position])) — verified live in db/custom.db (0 pre-existing rows in QA room); zero schema edits.
- API 1 `src/app/api/conversations/[id]/kanban/route.ts` (NEW): GET ?userId= → participantGuard (404 unknown room / 403 non-member, whiteboard pattern) → full snapshot { cards: [{id, conversationId, title, column, position, assigneeId, assigneeName, createdById, createdByName, createdAt, updatedAt}] }; SQL fetch ordered position asc then re-sorted in JS to board order todo→doing→done (alphabetical SQL would put "done" first) with createdAt+id tiebreaks; assignee/creator names resolved in ONE users-table query (Map). POST {userId, title 1..120, column? default 'todo', assigneeId?} → member check (403), assignee must be a room participant (400 otherwise), position = aggregate(_max position) + 1 per column → 201 { card }. Cache-Control no-store everywhere.
- API 2 `src/app/api/kanban/[cardId]/route.ts` (NEW): PATCH {userId, title?, column?, assigneeId? (null/'' clears), position?} → card 404 → member check against the CARD'S conversation (403) → partial update; column change with no explicit position = move-to-end (max in target column + 1); explicit integer position ≥ 0 wins; assignee-set re-validated as participant. DELETE ?userId= → 404/403 guards, then creator OR group-admin check (conversation.isGroup && participant.role === 'admin') → 403 with explicit message for member non-creators → { ok: true }.
- UI `src/components/chat/kanban-sheet.tsx` (NEW): vaul bottom sheet copied from whiteboard-sheet.tsx structure (DrawerContent rounded-t-3xl dark glass + sr-only DrawerTitle/Description + motion.div entrance will-change:transform) + role="dialog" aria-label="Kanban board". Header '📋 Board' with live total ('N cards · live' / Syncing… / Reconnecting… WifiOff) + close btn. Three side-by-side columns with tinted headers (todo zinc / doing amber / done emerald: colored dot + uppercase label + count chip), lists max-h-64 overflow-y-auto with the shared .pulse-scroll slim scrollbar, 'No cards' ghost states. Card rows: line-clamp-2 title (line-through+zinc when done), assignee chip = initials avatar on the member's gradient (members prop) + name, ← back / → forward quick-move (PATCH move-to-end), 🗑 delete rendered ONLY when canDelete(card) = card.createdById === meId || myRole === 'admin' (myRole prop defaults 'member'). Per-column footer input placeholder 'Add card…', Enter submits POST (pr-6 spinner while posting), maxLength 120.
- SYNC: TanStack useQuery ['kanban', conversationId], enabled while sheet open, refetchInterval fn → false when document.hidden (plus refetchIntervalInBackground:false) else 1500ms; mutations apiJson + invalidateQueries for instant refetch; per-card busy set locks buttons mid-flight; ApiError messages surfaced via sonner toasts; haptic ticks on add/move/delete/close.
- MOTION: motion.article layout + custom-indexed variants — entrance stagger (stagger(i, 0.05)) on spring.snappy, layout transitions on spring.snappy, exit scale-fade via AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" (deleted cards pop out while neighbors slide up); sheet body rides spring.soft; useReducedMotion collapses all of it to duration-0; will-change:transform on sheet wrapper + cards; transform/opacity only.
- EVENT CONTRACT: export const KANBAN_OPEN_EVENT = 'pulse:open-kanban'; export function useKanbanSheet(conversationId, meId, members, myRole='member') → { open, setOpen, node } — same 2-line wiring shape as useWhiteboardSheet (window listener opens the sheet).
- E2E (curl :3000, QA group cmtn1844z0000nhhcxv5i1f8p · Alice cmtawq3h4001ktcwn674m1frp admin · Bob cmtawqfwh001ltcwnsalt6659 member): ①Alice POST×3 → 201 (todo 'Design packet flow' default column+pos 0; doing 'Ship bot engine' assignee Bob → assigneeName "Bob" resolved; done 'R22 motion wave') ②GET as Alice → order [todo,doing,done] TRUE + assigneeName 'Bob' TRUE + createdByName 'Alice Chen' ③Bob PATCH 'Design packet flow' → doing → position 1 = max(0)+1 in doing, HTTP 200 ④Bob DELETE Alice's card → 403 "Only the card creator or a group admin…" ⑤Alice DELETE own card → {ok:true} 200 ⑥Bob GET foreign conv cmtay2eoj000itc5l02zhj3d0 (real conv he's not in, found via DB) → 403 "You are not a participant…" ⑦Cara (non-member) PATCH QA card → 403 ⑧title 121 chars → 400 ⑨assignee not participant → 400 ⑩missing userId → 400 ⑪bogus cardId → 404. FINAL STATE: doing 'Ship bot engine' (pos 0, Bob) · done 'R22 motion wave' (pos 0) — 'Design packet flow' deleted as scripted.

Task ID: R23-b
Agent: full-stack-developer (tic-tac-toe)
Task: Pulse in-chat games — playable tic-tac-toe matches that live in the chat as REAL kind:"game" messages. Zero mocks: GameMatch Prisma rows + 4 new REST routes + a self-polling glass card UI. Exclusive new files (no existing file touched): src/app/api/games/route.ts, src/app/api/games/[id]/route.ts, src/app/api/games/[id]/move/route.ts, src/app/api/games/[id]/join/route.ts, src/components/chat/game-tictactoe-card.tsx.

Work Log:
- Read worklog R21/R22 + copied established patterns verbatim: whiteboard participantGuard semantics, messages POST transaction (message + conversation.updatedAt bump + sender lastReadAt + unarchive-others), serializers mapMessage/MESSAGE_FULL_INCLUDE/notifySocket, motion.ts springs + fireParticles, glassSurface recipe, useQuery polling with refetchIntervalInBackground:false.
- SCHEMA: GameMatch was already pushed (board 9-char string, turn X|O, status active|x_won|o_won|draw|abandoned, playerOId null = open challenge, winLine JSON number[]). Conversation relation existed.
- POST /api/games {userId, conversationId, game?='tictactoe', opponentId?} → 201 {match, message}. Guards: 404 conv, 403 caller-not-participant, 400 opponent==self, 400 opponent-not-participant, 400 unknown game. Creates GameMatch{playerXId:userId, playerOId:opponentId??null} AND the invite Message{kind:'game', content:'⚔️ Tic-tac-toe challenge' | '⚔️ Tic-tac-toe — open challenge', payload:JSON {matchId, game:'tictactoe'}} inside one $transaction with the messages-route housekeeping bumps; relays socket 'message:new' to other members (mapMessage shape identical to messages API POST).
- GET /api/games?conversationId= → 400/404 guarded, {matches:[…]} newest-first take 25 (read-only, mirrors messages GET policy — reads unguarded, writes guarded).
- GET /api/games/[id] → {match, playerX:{id,name,color}, playerO:{id,name,color}|null} (404 if missing). winLine ships parsed number[]|null on the wire; board shipped as normalized 9-char string.
- POST /api/games/[id]/move {userId, cell 0..8} → 400 bad input · 404 · 409 not active · 403 not a player (side mapping X/O) · 409 'Not your turn.' · 409 'Cell taken.' · CONCURRENCY-SAFE: writes via guarded updateMany where {id, status:'active', turn:side, board:<pre-move snapshot>} so two racing moves can never both land (count 0 → 409 'Board changed — try again.'). Win detection = the 8 classic triples → status x_won/o_won + winnerId + winLine JSON, turn stays on winner; 9 moves no win → draw; else flip turn. Returns {match, playerX, playerO}.
- POST /api/games/[id]/join {userId} → only when active && playerOId null && userId!==playerXId && caller is room participant (403 else; 409 'already own'/'no longer open'); guarded updateMany(playerOId:null) makes double-join races deterministic → first wins, loser 409 'Someone else already took the O seat.' Returns {match, playerX, playerO}.
- game-tictactoe-card.tsx (default export {matchId, meId}): self-fetches detail via useQuery, polls every 1500ms ONLY while status==='active' (refetchInterval fn + refetchIntervalInBackground:false → cleared on unmount, paused when document.hidden); glass card (GlassCard + emerald glow, spring.soft entrance, will-change:transform, useReducedMotion-gated); header '⚔️ Tic-tac-toe' + Live/Finished chip; players line 'X <name> vs O <name>' with names tinted by the avatar color palette (emerald|rose|amber|violet|teal|orange|pink|cyan → verbatim tailwind text-*, dark variants); 3×3 aspect-square board, min 44px cells, aria-label 'Cell N', X/O glyphs pop with spring.bouncy tinted by their player's color; my turn → tappable (haptic + optimistic cell fill at 60% opacity, POST, setQueryData; error → invalidate + toast with the server's message); not my turn → disabled + 'Waiting for NAME…' shimmer (opacity keyframes, reduced-motion → static); open challenge && meId!==playerXId → emerald 'Join as O' button (also X-owner sees 'waiting for opponent' shimmer); finished → status banner '<Name> wins!' with Trophy / 'Draw — board is full.', winning triple ring-2 emerald + others dimmed; when I win fireParticles({kind:'confetti', count:120}) EXACTLY ONCE per mount (ref-guarded against poll refetches, reduced-motion skip); 'Rematch' (finished && I am a player) → POST /api/games same room, opponent = other player, then window.dispatchEvent CustomEvent 'pulse:external-message' {detail: res.message}; exports GAME_EXTERNAL_MESSAGE_EVENT for the lead.
- VERIFY (all curl, :3000, real DB): see transcript below.

VERIFY:
- Ids: Alice cmtawq3h4001ktcwn674m1frp · Bob cmtawqfwh001ltcwnsalt6659 · bot cmtbdcvxd0000tcuzdqql8mim · DM cmtawqnzg001mtcwn0h4uwzhr (Alice+Bob) · QA group cmtn1844z0000nhhcxv5i1f8p (Alice+Bob+bot).

- Guards: move on finished → 409 'This match is not active anymore.' · cell 9 → 400 · bot (room participant of QA, NOT a player) move on ACTIVE QA match → 403 'You are not a player in this match.'
- Open challenge (QA): POST without opponentId → matchId cmtn7rs420005nhyn7tu9q7hj, content '⚔️ Tic-tac-toe — open challenge', playerOId null; Bob join → playerOId=Bob; join again → 409 'no longer open'; turn flip proven (X0 → turn O; out-of-turn Alice → 409 'Not your turn.'); cell-taken proven (Alice taps O@1 → 409 'Cell taken.'); Bob win → o_won winnerId=Bob winLine=[1,4,7] (board "XO XO  OX"); move after end → 409.

- API 1/3 `src/app/api/conversations/[id]/events/route.ts` (NEW): GET ?userId= → participantGuard (404 conv / 403 non-member, same pattern as whiteboard route) → two bounded findMany queries (upcoming startsAt>=now asc, past desc, take 50 each) merged upcoming-first capped 50 → events serialized with rsvps [{userId,name,status}], counts {going,maybe,no}, myStatus (from the userId query param), createdByName resolved via one batched user findMany (no creator relation in schema — resolved by id map, null-safe). POST {userId,title(1..120),startsAt(ISO Date.parse),description?≤1000,location?≤200} → member guard → 201 {event} with rsvps:[] (creator does NOT auto-RSVP).
- API 2/3 `src/app/api/events/[id]/rsvp/route.ts` (NEW): POST {userId,status} → event 404 → membership 403 → status whitelist going|maybe|no (400) → Prisma upsert on compound key eventId_userId → REVOVE MOVES the row (same row id, status updated) → returns {rsvp, counts} with a fresh post-upsert tally.
- API 3/3 `src/app/api/events/[id]/route.ts` (NEW): DELETE ?userId= → event 404 → membership 403 → permission = creator OR participant.role==='admin' (else 403 with actionable message) → delete (EventRsvp cascades) → {ok:true}.
- UI `src/components/chat/events-sheet.tsx` (NEW): exact whiteboard-sheet wiring — vaul Drawer (handleOnly) + DrawerContent glass `rounded-t-3xl` mx-auto max-w-[420px] h-[92dvh] dark zinc-950 + sr-only DrawerTitle('Events')/DrawerDescription + motion.div spring entrance with will-change:transform + header (emerald CalendarDays tile, live status dot: emerald pulse / rose 'Reconnecting…', close X). Body = pulse-scroll flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto (custom thin scrollbar, safe-area pb). CREATE FORM on top: title input (maxLength 120), datetime-local ([color-scheme:dark], default = tomorrow 18:00 local), location input, 'Schedule event' submit (emerald, spinner while pending) → POST + invalidate + success toast; spring entrance. UPCOMING rows: stagger variants (listVariants 0.05 / rowVariants spring.soft), AnimatePresence exits, layout springs; each row = calendar-tile date badge (short month + day number), title, live countdown chip recomputed by a 30s self-chaining setTimeout tick ('in 2h 15m' → emerald; 'Starting now' <60s → solid emerald fill; past rows get zinc '2d ago' chip), MapPin location line, 'by NAME' attribution (members map first, API createdByName fallback), RSVP pill row Going n/Maybe n/Can't n — tappable POST with OPTIMISTIC onMutate cache patch (my pill fills emerald instantly, counts re-tally, rollback+toast on error, invalidate on settle), going-member initials avatar stack (member colors via gradientFor, 4 max + '+n', ring-zinc-950 overlap). PAST section collapsed by default (aria-expanded toggle, ChevronDown rotate, AnimatePresence height auto/0 spring). DELETE trash per row, visible only to creator or admin (myRole prop), two-tap confirm (2.6s armed state, rose fill) → DELETE + invalidate. POLL: TanStack useQuery refetchInterval 5000 + refetchIntervalInBackground:false (auto-pauses when document.hidden). a11y: role dialog labelled 'Events' (aria-label + sr-only title), RSVP buttons aria-label `RSVP going|maybe|no ${title}` + aria-pressed, delete `Delete event ${title}` (and 'Tap again…' armed), aria-labels on every form field, GoingStack role="img" with count label. Motion: spring presets from @/lib/motion only, pressTap on pills/trash, useReducedMotion gates entrance/layout/press everywhere.

Bob RSVP going → counts going=1. Alice RSVP maybe → going=1/maybe=1; Alice re-RSVP going → SAME rsvp id cmtn7tl8d000xnhynout9wv31 (revote moved the row) counts going=2/maybe=0. GET as Alice → myStatus 'going', counts {going:2,maybe:0,no:0}, names resolved. Task ID: R23-a
Agent: full-stack-developer (red packets)
Task: WeChat-style red packets — real PC economy grabs in chat (create/grab/detail + lazy refund, bubble + composer sheet)

Work Log:
- Read worklog (R21/R22 contracts) + reference files (whiteboard route/sheet, wallet transfer, messages route, serializers, motion.ts, glass-card.tsx) and copied established patterns exactly (safeJson/strField, interactive $transaction with sentinel errors, mapMessage + MESSAGE_FULL_INCLUDE, Drawer sheet + useXxxSheet hook, spring/ease tokens, notifySocket relay).
- POST /api/redpackets: validates userId/conversationId present, total int 1..10000, count int 1..50, count ≤ total (every slice ≥ 1 PC), sender is ConversationParticipant (404/403 semantics like messages route). ONE tx: wallet upsert + funds check (402) + decrement (negative guard) → cut-the-rope split (count-1 DISTINCT random cuts in [1,total-1], sorted, diffs = slices → sum === total, each ≥ 1) → Message row kind 'redpacket' content note||'🧧 Red packet' → RedPacket row (expiresAt = +24h) → Message.payload patched with real packetId ({packetId,total,count,note}) → conversation updatedAt bump + sender lastReadAt + archive unpark (messages-route parity) → WalletLedger 'redpacket_send' -total → LogEvent 'sent a red packet of N PC'. Returns 201 { message: <mapMessage output, byte-identical shape to messages POST>, packet: {id,total,count,grabbed,note,expiresAt} } + notifySocket('message:new') to other members.
- GET /api/redpackets/[id]?userId=: returns packet{id,senderId,total,count,grabbed,note,expiresAt,status:'open'|'exhausted'|'expired'}, senderName, grabs ordered amount DESC/createdAt ASC (WeChat best-luck), myGrab, isMine. LAZY REFUND: first reader after expiry settles leftovers in one tx (re-read guard → idempotent): refund sum(slices[grabbed..]) to sender wallet + WalletLedger 'redpacket_refund' + 'expired red packet refund' + LogEvent + grabbed=count (closes packet); status computed after refund. RedPacket has NO direct User relation in schema → names resolved via one db.user.findMany (no schema edits).
- POST /api/redpackets/[id]/grab: ONE tx enforces packet exists (404), not expired (400), grabber is room participant (403), grabber ≠ sender (400 'You cannot grab your own red packet.'), grabbed < count (400), no (packetId,userId) grab row (400) → slices[grabbed] drawn under a compare-and-set updateMany (grabbed = read-value → increment) so racing grabs can't double-draw → grab row + wallet credit + WalletLedger 'redpacket_claim' (+amount, counterpartyId=sender) + LogEvent → { amount, grabbed, count }.
- redpacket-bubble.tsx (default export, props {packetId,meId,mine,senderName}): self-fetches detail via TanStack useQuery (['redpacket',packetId,meId], 20s refetch), amber-500→rose-500 gradient card + large 🧧 + note + status line per contract (mine → 'N of M grabbed — tap for details'; grabbed → '+N PC'; open → 'Tap to open'; exhausted → 'All grabbed'; expired → 'Expired'); tap when openable (status open & !mine & myGrab null) POSTs grab → cache patch → spring rotateY flip reveal (spring.bouncy, will-change transform) + fireParticles({kind:'burst',count:90}) + haptic; owners/claimants tap → inline grabs ledger (glass surface, initial-circle rows, Crown 'Lucky' badge on max, 'Total N PC · x/y grabbed' footer, max-h-40 scroll); aria-label 'Red packet' section + 'Grab red packet' button; useReducedMotion strips flips/keyframes/stagger.
- redpacket-sheet.tsx: bottom sheet on the whiteboard Drawer pattern (DrawerContent max-w-[420px] zinc-950, motion.form spring.soft, sr-only title/description, safe-area padding); amount input (1..10000 PC), 1..50 stepper (44px buttons), 60-char note with counter, live preview 'N PC · k grabs · ≈ avg PC each' + REAL balance via GET /api/hub/wallet with insufficient warning; submit → POST /api/redpackets → close + toast '🧧 Red packet sent' + window.dispatchEvent(CustomEvent('pulse:external-message', {detail: res.message})). Exports RedPacketSheet, useRedPacketSheet(conversationId, meId) → {open,setOpen,node} listening on REDPACKET_OPEN_EVENT='pulse:open-redpacket'.
- E2E proofs (real curl :3000): QA topup fixture set Alice (cmtawq3h4001ktcwn674m1frp) wallet 10→500 PC + qa_topup ledger +490 (bun script /tmp/qa-redpacket-topup.ts). Validation: total=0→400, count>total→400, count=51→400, total=10000 vs 500→402 'Insufficient PC — you have 500, tried to send 10000.'. Created packet cmtn7sxwf000hnhyntqbbv9rj (30 PC ÷ 3, note 'Lucky draw') → message cmtn7sxw7000fnhynttqbbz1j confirmed GET-able via messages API with IDENTICAL ChatMessage shape (kind 'redpacket', payload {packetId,total,count,note}). Bob (cmtawqfwh001ltcwnsalt6659) grabbed 6 PC (grabbed 1/3, 6≤28 ✓); re-grab → 400 'You already grabbed this red packet.'; Alice grab → 400 'You cannot grab your own red packet.'; Cara (real user, non-member) → 403 participant guard. Slices [6,5,19]: sum=30 ✓ each≥1 ✓ len=3 ✓ (/tmp/qa-redpacket-slices.ts). Exhausted path: packet cmtn7to7i0013nhyn77gsm4zv (2 PC × 1) grabbed by Bob → status 'exhausted', myGrab=2. QA group has no third real member (only Alice/Bot@pulseai/Bot) → per brief asserted grabbed+slices math via detail GET instead of a third grab. Lazy refund: packet cmtn7u397001jnhyn4kpiyknt (5 PC × 2, ungrabbed) backdated -60s via /tmp/qa-redpacket-expire.ts → next GET refunded 5 PC (463→468), grabbed 0→2 closed, status 'expired', ledger redpacket_refund +5, SECOND GET idempotent (coins still 468). - user-avatar.tsx (shared, ADDITIVE ONLY): new optional `avatar?: string | null` prop — when truthy renders `<AvatarImage src className="size-full rounded-full object-cover">` above the existing palette `AvatarFallback` (Radix shows fallback while loading / on broken path, so surfaces can never render a black hole); existing name/color/size/online/showPresence props and all ~50 call sites stay 100% backward-compatible (falsy avatar → palette glyph exactly as before). Added the subtle glass rim ring shared with the design language: an absolutely-positioned overlay span with `boxShadow: var(--glass-rim)` (inline style → light/dark token swap works without JIT risk) on EVERY avatar, photo or palette.
- NEW src/components/profile/avatar-editor.tsx — `AvatarPhotoEditor({me})` placed at the TOP of the #/profile/edit form body (above Identity): 88px circular preview in the hero's gradient-ring + glass-rim treatment (photo when set, palette initials otherwise, spring.bouncy pop on change via key remount); "Set photo"/"Replace photo" → hidden `<input type="file" accept="image/*">` (#avatar-photo-input); pick → canvas CENTER-CROP to square + downscale ≤512px (never upscale) → JPEG quality 0.85 data URL (transparency flattened white) → optimistic local preview shows the compressed dataUrl immediately → POST /api/uploads → PATCH /api/users/[id] {avatar:`/api/uploads/${filePath}`} → session store setUser + setQueryData(['me',id]) + setQueryData(['users']) map + invalidateQueries(['me',id])/(['users']) refetch → sonner success toast. "Remove photo" (rendered only when avatar set) → PATCH {avatar:""} → same cache write → toast. Distinct live phase copy (Optimizing/Uploading/Saving…, aria-live=polite) + spinning scrim over the preview + buttons disabled while busy; every failure = sonner toast AND the optimistic preview resets (never a broken image); haptic(10) + pressTap springs on all buttons; 44px touch targets; sr-only input + aria-labels; value reset after pick so the same file can be re-picked.

Wallpaper options typed as PulsePrefs['wallpaper'] (compile error if the global set drifts); tint set per task spec (emerald|rose|amber|violet|teal); sanitizer drops unknown ids/tokens (regex key guard + __proto__/constructor/prototype rejection) and caps at 48 entries to stay under the API's 4KB serialized-prefs budget
- OWNERSHIP EXCEPTION (additive, justified): edited src/lib/prefs-defaults.ts — mergePrefs is a strict whitelist shared by BOTH the client store (save() strips unknown keys before set) and the server PATCH clamp, so without registering the key there, 'chat.convThemes' would be silently dropped on the very first save AND every server write. Added: 'chat.convThemes'?: ConvThemeMap field + one mergePrefs branch (isRecord → sanitizeConvThemeMap). Not on the do-not-touch list; mirrors the existing fx.webglMode pattern. RoomInfoPageProps untouched. Reads prefs via usePrefsValues so the row + picker update live
- chat-room.tsx SURGICAL (3 lines + import): the single wallpaperGlows(prefs.wallpaper, isDark) call now resolves effectiveConvWallpaper(prefs, conversationId) and applyConvTint(..., tint) (tint replaces the TOP glow so it is visible even on the 'none' wallpaper; bottom glow keeps wallpaper character). Nothing else restructured; re-render rides the existing usePrefsValues subscription

- Prefs key contract: User.preferences['chat.convThemes'] = Record<conversationId, {wallpaper: 'none'|'aurora'|'dusk'|'forest'|'mono', tint?: 'emerald'|'rose'|'amber'|'violet'|'teal'}> — sanitized by sanitizeConvThemeMap (max 48 entries, 4KB-safe), passthrough registered in mergePrefs; tint = top-glow color swap in chat-room (no global tint concept exists)
- Honest gaps: (1) tint-only overrides seed wallpaper from the global default at set-time (entry type requires wallpaper) — later global changes do not propagate to seeded rooms; (2) reset leaves an empty {} map in the blob (semantically correct, tiny residue); (3) per-user prefs = per-user themes (the OTHER party does not see your override — by design, client-side pref like iMessage); (4) 48-override cap is silent (drop-beyond-cap)

- ROOT CAUSE (highest-probability, actionable): minSdk 26 → AGP/apksigner defaults ship v2/v3-only APKs (no v1 JAR signature); picky ROM installers (MIUI/HyperOS class) reject v2-only archives with exactly "There was a problem parsing the package".
